- **p-value-combination-fisher-method**: Use when when you have tabulated p-values and study sample sizes (N) from multiple independent metabolomics studies measuring the same compounds or metabolites, and you need to compute a single meta-analytic p-value that reflects both statistical significance and study weight. - **p-value-cutoff-threshold-filtering**: Use when after differential expression analysis (DEA) has produced p-values and log fold-change values for multiple omics layers (genes, miRNA, proteins, lipids), and you need to select only statistically significant features for pathway enrichment.
